# Donor-linked attacks and bio-perturbation experiments: tables

Generated by `scripts/perturb/report.py`; the reading of these tables is in `docs/DONOR_LINKED_AND_PERTURBATIONS.md`.

## Donor-linked membership inference

### How recognisable is a donor's second sample?

For each second sample: where does its own donor's challenge sample rank among all candidates by correlation (no generator involved).

| tier                                                | dataset   |   n |   own_donor_is_nearest |   own_donor_in_top10 |   median_rank |   corr_to_own_A |   corr_to_best_other |
|:----------------------------------------------------|:----------|----:|-----------------------:|---------------------:|--------------:|----------------:|---------------------:|
| matched normal                                      | BRCA      | 111 |                  0     |                0.027 |         392   |           0.073 |                0.802 |
| other lesion (metastatic / recurrent / new primary) | BRCA      |   7 |                  0.571 |                0.571 |           1   |           0.579 |                0.563 |
| same tumour sample, other aliquot                   | BRCA      |   3 |                  1     |                1     |           1   |           0.819 |                0.593 |
| same tumour, other vial                             | BRCA      |   6 |                  0.5   |                0.833 |           1.5 |           0.698 |                0.641 |
| matched normal                                      | COMBINED  | 410 |                  0.017 |                0.051 |         331   |           0.352 |                0.812 |
| other lesion (metastatic / recurrent / new primary) | COMBINED  |  25 |                  0.68  |                0.76  |           1   |           0.738 |                0.66  |

### Attack results

`linked` = AUC for the donor's membership from the second sample (never trained on); `overlap` = the same donors' trained-on samples; brackets are 95% donor-bootstrap intervals; `within donor` = how often a second sample scores higher in a split where its donor is a member than in the split where it is not (0.5 = no effect).

### MeLoMIA (second samples read through the existing shadow stacks)

`linked` = AUC for the donor's membership from the second sample (never trained on); `overlap` = the same donors' trained-on samples; brackets are 95% donor-bootstrap intervals; `within donor` = how often a second sample scores higher in a split where its donor is a member than in the split where it is not (0.5 = no effect).
